    OVERVIEW                                                             概覽
    We are the leading and a rapidly growing home meal products          我們是中國領先且快速增長的在家吃飯餐食
    brand in China. We offer a variety of ready-to-eat, ready-to-heat,   產品品牌。我們提供即食、即熱、即煮和即
    ready-to-cook and prepared ingredients, with a focus on at-home      配食材，並專注於在家火鍋和燒烤產品。憑
    hotpot and barbecue products. With a carefully curated product       藉精心策劃的產品組合和廣泛的即時零售門
    portfolio and an extensive network of instant retail stores,         店網絡，我們為消費者提供產品，使他們能
    we enable consumers to enjoy meals at home with tastiness,           夠在家中享用好吃、方便還不貴的餐食產
    convenience and affordability. Leveraging our robust supply          品。憑藉我們強大的供應鏈能力以及遍佈全
    chain and production capabilities and a nationwide network of        國的零售門店，我們使用鍋圈食匯品牌提供
    retail stores, we offer a wide variety of home meal products under   各種餐食產品，服務於不同的用餐場景。
    the Guoquan Shihui (鍋圈食匯) brand, serving diverse dining
    scenarios.


    We are devoted to developing products under our proprietary          我們致力於開發帶有「鍋圈食匯」標誌的自有
    brand name carrying the “Guoquan Shihui” logo. As the leading        品牌產品。作為中國領先的一站式在家吃飯
    one-stop home meal products brand in China, we are well              餐食產品品牌，我們具備把握中國在家吃飯
    positioned to capture the significant growth potential in China’s    餐食產品市場巨大增長潛力的能力，並以優
    home meal products market. We cater to different dining              質食材為基礎，具有創造新產品及多樣化產
    scenarios underpinned by high quality food ingredients that have     品的巨大潛力，滿足不同的用餐場景。我們
    an immense potential for creating new and diverse products. Our      的產品組合包括八大類別，涵蓋火鍋產品、
    product portfolio comprises eight categories including hotpot        燒烤產品、飲品、一人食、即烹餐包、生
    products, barbecue products, beverages, solo-dining meals,           鮮、西餐及零食。
    ready-to-cook meal kits, fresh produce, western cuisines and
    snacks.


    We have established a network of instant retail stores primarily     我們已建立主要包括加盟店的即時零售門店
    comprising franchised stores. Under our franchise business           網絡。在我們的特許經營業務模式下，我們
    model, we derive substantially all of our revenue from the sale of   的絕大部分收益來自向加盟商銷售我們的產
    our products to franchisees, who open and operate franchised         品，加盟商以我們的品牌開設及經營加盟店
    stores under our brand and sell our products to consumers. We        並向消費者銷售我們的產品。我們並不收取
    do not charge or rely on franchise fees.                             亦不依賴於加盟費。


    We have also been developing online sales channels including our     我們亦開發線上銷售渠道，包括我們的鍋圈
    Guoquan APP, WeChat mini-program as well as on popular social        APP、微信小程序以及流行社交商務平台（如
    commerce platforms such as Douyin. In addition, we partner with      抖音）。此外，我們與美团及餓了麼等第三
    third-party food delivery platforms, such as Meituan and Ele.me      方外賣平台合作，為消費者提供外賣到家服
    to deliver our products to consumers. We achieve highly extensive    務。線上線下的購物選擇，實現了廣泛的消
    consumer reach by providing both online and offline shopping         費者觸達。憑藉供應鏈及數字化管理體系的
    options. Leveraging the highly efficient management and              高效管理及運營，我們能夠確保產品質量及
    operation of our supply chain and our digitalized management         安全並實現從食品生產到零售的高運營效率。
    system, we are able to ensure product quality and safety, as well
    as achieve high operational efficiency from food production to
    retail sales.

OUR VISION                                                         我們的願景
Our vision is to become the go-to brand for dining at home in      我們的願景是在家吃就鍋圈。
China.


OUR MISSION                                                        我們的使命
Our mission is to source quality food ingredients globally and     我們的使命是匯聚全球好食材，多場景提供
offer diverse, convenient, high-quality and value-for-money home   老百姓一站式在家吃飯「多、快、好、省」的
meal products in a one-stop shop manner to meet consumers’         餐食產品，讓偏遠鄉村的老百姓也能吃到鍋
diverse needs under different dining scenarios, from urban         圈好吃方便還不貴的好食材。
centers to the most remote areas of China.

results of operations, and business prospects may be affected by      業務前景可能受到本集團業務直接或間接相

to the Group’s business. Some of the major risks that we face         的若干主要風險包括：(i)不論是在現有市場
include: (i) Awareness, recognition and popularity of our brand,      還是新市場，我們品牌的知名度、認可度以
whether in our existing markets or new markets, are pivotal to        及受歡迎程度均對我們業務的成功至關重
the success of our business. Any negative impacts on our brand        要。我們的品牌或聲譽受到任何負面影響或
or reputation, or failure to effectively promote our brand, could     未能有效推廣我們的品牌均可能對我們的業
adversely affect our business and results of operations; (ii) Our     務及經營業績產生不利影響；(ii)我們的業務
business is affected by changes in consumer tastes and dining         受到消費者口味和飲食偏好變化的影響，我
preference, which we may not be able to anticipate and identify       們可能無法及時或根本無法預測和識別這種
in a timely manner or at all. Our efforts in developing, launching    變化。我們在開發、推出和推廣新產品以及
and promoting new products, and diversifying our product and          實現產品組合及品牌組合多樣化方面的努力
brand portfolio may not be successful, which may expose us to         可能不會取得成功，這可能會使我們面臨額
the risks of extra costs and expenses; (iii) Any failure by us, our   外成本及開支風險；(iii)我們或者我們的加
franchisees or our suppliers to maintain effective quality control    盟商或供應商如果不能維持有效的產品質量
systems of our products could have a material adverse effect on       監控制度，可能會對我們的品牌聲譽、業務
our brand reputation, business and operations; (iv) Our extensive     及運營造成重大不利影響；(iv) 我們廣泛的
store network primarily comprises franchised stores that are          門店網絡主要包括由加盟商經營的加盟店。
operated by franchisees. Our results of operations are largely        加盟店的業績對我們的經營業績產生重大影
subject to the performance of the franchised stores. We cannot        響。我們無法控制及可能無法有效監察該等
control and may not be able to effectively monitor the operations     加盟店的運營或維持我們與加盟商的現有關

     RESPONSIBILITIES OF AND DELEGATION BY THE                             董事會責任及授權
     BOARD
     The Board is responsible for the overall leadership of the Group,     董事會負責本集團的整體領導工作，監督本
     oversees the Group’s strategic decisions, and monitors business       集團的戰略決策及監控業務和表現。
     and performance.


     The management, including executive Directors, is delegated with      管理層（包括執行董事）獲授權負責實施董事
     responsibilities for implementing the strategy and direction as       會不時採納的策略及方針，以及負責本集團
     adopted by the Board from time to time, and conducting the day-       的日常營運，定期會面，以檢討本集團整體
     to-day operations of the Group. They meet regularly to review         業務表現、統籌整體資源及作出財務及營運
     the performance of the businesses of the Group as a whole,            決策。董事會亦就其管理權力（包括管理層應
     coordinate overall resources and make financial and operational       匯報的情況）作出明確指示，並將定期檢討授
     decisions. The Board also gives clear directions as to their powers   權安排，以確保其仍然切合本集團的需要。
     of management including circumstances where management
     should report back, and will review the delegation arrangements
     on a periodic basis to ensure that they remain appropriate to the
     needs of the Group.

     To better serve the long-term interests of our stakeholders,        為使我們的持份者更能達致長期的利益，董

     committees as they require independent oversight and specialist     由委員會處理更為適宜。因此，董事會已成
     input. As such, the Board has established three committees          立三個委員會，並已向委員會（包括審核與風
     and has delegated various responsibilities to the committees        險管理委員會、薪酬委員會及提名委員會授
     including Audit and Risk Management Committee, Remuneration         予多項職責。所有董事委員會均根據其各自
     Committee and Nomination Committee. All the Board committees        的職權範圍（可於本公司及聯交所網站查閱）
     perform their distinct roles in accordance with their respective    履行各自的職責。董事委員會獲提供充足資
     terms of reference which are available on the websites of the       源以履行其職責，並可應合理要求於適當情

     BOARD DIVERSITY                                                       董事會多元化
     The Company views enhancing diversity at the Board level as           本公司視提升董事會層面的多元化為實現集
     a key element in achieving the Group’s strategic objectives           團戰略目標及達致可持續均衡發展的關鍵元
     and attaining sustainable and balanced development. The               素。本公司已採納董事會多元化政策（「董事
     Company has adopted a board diversity policy (the “Board              會多元化政策」），該政策強調董事會成員多
     Diversity Policy”), which emphasizes the benefits of Board            元化對於董事會效能和決策程序、董事會的
     member diversity for Board effectiveness and decision-making          繼任計劃和發展以及實現本公司戰略目標等
     processes, Board succession planning and development, and the         方面的裨益，並列明董事會對於在性別方面
     achievement of the Company’s strategic objectives, and also sets      以及其他不同層面達致多元化所作出的承諾。
     out the Board’s commitment to achieving diversity in terms of
     gender and other different levels.


     Pursuant to the Board Diversity Policy, the Company promotes          根據董事會多元化政策，本公司在甄選董事
     diversity in Board members considering a number of factors when       會候選人時，會綜合考慮多項因素以促進董
     selecting candidates to the Board, including but not limited to       事會成員組成的多元化，包括但不限於專業
     professional experience, skills, knowledge, gender, age, cultural     經驗、技能、知識、性別、年齡、文化、教
     and education background, nationality and length of service. The      育背景、民族及服務年期等方面。董事會將
     Board will promote a reasonable balance in the gender structure       參考國際及本地相關最佳實踐，並關注持份
     of the Board with reference to relevant international and local       者的合理期望，以推動董事會性別結構的合
     best practices and considering the reasonable expectations            理平衡。在此基礎上，董事會設定以下目
     of stakeholders. On this basis, the Board has set the following       標：(i)女性董事比例及董事繼任人庫中女性
     targets: (i) the proportion of female directors and the proportion    候選人比例均不低於三分之一，以此作為現
     of female candidates among director successors shall both be no       階段的具體目標；(ii)以最終實現董事會性別
     less than one-third, as specific targets at the current stage; (ii)   均衡為長遠目標。
     the long-term goal is to ultimately achieve gender balance of the
     Board.


     As of the Latest Practicable Date, the Board comprises four           截至最後實際可行日期，董事會共有四名女

NOMINATION POLICY AND PROCEDURES                                      提名政策及程序
The Company has also adopted policies and procedures for              本公司亦採納提名選舉董事的政策及程序
nomination and election of Directors (the “Nomination Policy”).       （「提名政策」）。該提名政策訂有選舉及績效
The Nomination Policy sets out the criteria and procedures for        評估的標準與程序，為董事會提供有關董事
selection and performance evaluation and provides guidance to         提名及委任的指引。董事會認為，清晰的選
the Board on the nomination and appointment of Directors. The         舉流程便於企業管治，可確保董事會的連續
Board believes that a clear selection process facilitates corporate   性，維持董事會的領導地位，提高董事會效
governance, ensures the continuity of the Board, maintains            率及多元化。提名委員會及董事會均可提名
the leadership of the Board, and enhances the efficiency and          董事候選人。評估推薦候選人是否合適及對
diversity of the Board. Both the Nomination Committee and the         董事會的潛在貢獻時，提名委員會可參考誠
Board can nominate candidates for Directors. When evaluating          信度、專業資質及技能、知識、經驗、承諾
the suitability of recommended candidates and their potential         及相關貢獻等若干選舉標準。提名委員會須
contributions to the Board, the Nomination Committee may              就委任合適的董事候選人向董事會報告評估
refer to certain selection criteria such as integrity, professional   結果並提供相關建議，供董事會決策及制定
qualifications and skills, knowledge, experience, commitment and      整體選舉計劃，並於股東會上提呈供股東批
related contributions. The Nomination Committee shall report the      准。
evaluation results to the Board on the appointment of suitable
Director candidates and provide relevant recommendations to
the Board to make decisions and formulate a general election
plan which should be proposed at the general meeting for the
Shareholders’ approval.

The Company has established the mechanism to ensure                   本公司已建立機制以確保董事會可獲取獨立
independent views and input are available to the Board. The           觀點和意見。董事會確保委任最少三名獨立
Board ensures the appointment of at least three independent           非執行董事及當中最少三分之一成員為獨立
non-executive Directors and at least one-third of its members         非執行董事。此外，本公司亦會按上市規則
being independent non-executive Directors. Further, independent       的規定及實際可行情況委任獨立非執行董事
non-executive Directors will be appointed to the Board                加入董事委員會，以確保獲取獨立觀點和意
Committees as required under the Listing Rules and as far as          見。提名委員會嚴格遵守上市規則所載有關
practicable to ensure independent views and input are available.      提名及委任獨立非執行董事的獨立性評估準
The Nomination Committee strictly adheres to the independence         則，並獲授權每年評估獨立非執行董事的獨
assessment criteria as set out in the Listing Rules with regard to    立性，確保能持續作出獨立判斷。獨立非執
the nomination and appointment of independent non-executive           行董事並無給予帶有績效表現相關元素的股
Directors, and is mandated to assess annually the independence        本權益薪酬。最後，董事（包括獨立非執行董
of independent non-executive Directors to ensure that they            事）如於合約或安排中擁有重大利益，則不得
can continually exercise independent judgement. No equity-            就通過該合約或安排的董事決議案投票，亦
based remuneration with performance-related elements will             不得計入法定人數。
be granted to independent non-executive Directors. Last, a
Director (including independent non-executive Director) who has

Primary Risks                                                             主要風險

Given the Company’s pivotal role in the “integrated production,           基於本公司在火鍋食材行業「產供銷一體化」
supply and marketing” supply chain of the hotpot ingredients              的供應鏈重要地位，集團面對多項主要風險
industry, the Group faces a number of principal risks and                 及不明朗因素，倘沒有妥善管理，可能會為
uncertainties that if not properly managed could create an                集團帶來影響。全面的風險評估（包括但不限
exposure for the Group. Thorough risk assessment (including               於財務、業務及戰略、營運、ESG及法律合
but not limited to financial, business and strategic, operational,        規風險）及紓減風險措施有助集團確保該等風
ESG, and legal compliance risks) and mitigation help ensure               險得到適當管理及有效控制。於報告期間，
these risks are well managed and governed effectively. During the         集團集中解決以下主要風險：
Reporting Period, the Group focused on addressing the following
primary risks:

 Type of Risks        Countermeasures

 風險類型                 應對策略

 Food safety risk     •     Enhance the food safety management system and have established a food safety team
                            to collaborate with relevant departments, and proactively identify, control, and eliminate
                            food safety hazards to ensure product quality, safeguard brand reputation, and protect the
                            collective interests of our franchise partners.

 食品安全風險               •     完善食品安全管理體系，建立食品安全小組聯合各相關業務部門推動食品安全工作共治，及時發
                            現、控制、消除食品安全隱患，保證產品質量，保護品牌聲譽，維護廣大加盟商的共同利益。

 Production           •     Improve the production safety management system, conduct regular safety inspections,
   safety risk              identify and assess production risks, develop targeted control measures, and strengthen
                            safety training for employees to improve awareness and response capabilities.

 安全生產風險               •     完善安全生產管理體系，定期開展安全隱患排查，識別、評估生產過程中的風險，針對各項風險
                            積極制定管控策略；同時加強員工安全生產培訓，提高全員安全意識及安全風險應對能力。

 Environmental risk   •     Strengthen the environmental management systems of the Company and its subsidiaries,
                            standardise environmental management processes, implement clean production strategies,
                            strictly control emissions of wastewater, waste gas, and solid waste, and formulate
                            contingency plans for environmental emergencies.

 環境風險                 •     推動公司及附屬公司完善環境管理體系，規範環境管理流程，實行清潔生產策略，嚴格控制三廢
                            排放。同時，制定應急預案，應對突發環境事件。

 Financial risk       •     Standardise related-party transactions and reduce unnecessary transactions to ensure
                            fairness and impartiality in decision-making.

                      •     Engage external auditors annually for audits of financial reports and relevant internal
                            controls, and urge the Audit and Investigation Centre to inspect operations and financial
                            records to ensure compliance and accuracy.

                      •     Strictly comply with local tax regulations, improve tax-related systems and procedures, and
                            rigorously prevent tax-related risks.

 財務風險                 •     規範關聯交易行為和減少不必要的關聯交易，保證關聯交易決策對所有股東的公平性和公正性。

                      •     每年聘請外部審計對我們財務及相關內部控制進行審計，同時審計調查中心檢查業務操作和財務
                            記錄，確保合規性和準確性。

                      •     嚴格遵守各運營地稅務相關法律法規，完善稅務管理相關制度，規範稅務管理流程，嚴格防範稅
                            務風險。

 Legal compliance     •     Have established monitoring systems to continuously oversee key risk areas and business
   risk                     processes, promptly identify irregularities, and regularly review and update policies and
                            procedures to adapt to business and regulatory changes.

 法律合規風險               •     建立監控系統，持續監控關鍵風險點和業務流程，及時發現異常情況；並定期審查和更新政策和
                            程序，以適應業務變化和法規更新。

       Type of Risks       Countermeasures

       風險類型                應對策略

       Information         •      Adopt advanced technologies and tools such as firewalls, security software, and encryption
         security risk            technologies to mitigate technical risks related to information security.

                           •      Conduct specialised risk self-assessment of core business system every six months in
                                  accordance with the requirements of the Group’s information security system documents.

       信息安全風險              •      採用先進的技術和設備，如防火牆、安全軟件、加密技術等，預防信息安全相關技術風險。

                           •      每半年基於集團信息安全體系文件要求對核心業務系統開展專項風險管理自查工作。

       Other operational   •      To address potential market fluctuations and compliance risks in the sales process, the
         risks                    Company implemented strategic measures by deepening market research and dynamically
                                  optimizing product portfolios and pricing strategies to ensure timely responses to market
                                  demands; at the operational level, it enhanced sales process standards and intensified staff
                                  training to continuously improve the sales team’s professional competence and execution
                                  capabilities, thereby effectively mitigating risks associated with monopolistic practices and
                                  unfair competition.

       其他運營風險              •      針對銷售環節可能出現的市場波動及合規隱患，公司在策略層面，通過深化市場調研，動態優化
                                  產品組合與定價策略，以確保及時響應市場需求；在執行層面，通過完善銷售流程規範及加強人
                                  員培訓，持續提升銷售團隊的專業素養與執行力，有效防範壟斷及不正當競爭風險。

AUDITOR’S RESPONSIBILITIES FOR THE AUDIT OF                            核數師就審計綜合財務報表承擔的責
THE CONSOLIDATED FINANCIAL STATEMENTS                                  任
Our objectives are to obtain reasonable assurance about whether        我們的目標，是對綜合財務報表整體是否不
the consolidated financial statements as a whole are free from         存在由於欺詐或錯誤而導致的重大錯誤陳述
material misstatement, whether due to fraud or error, and to           取得合理鑒證，並出具載有我們意見的核數
issue an auditor’s report that includes our opinion. Our report is     師報告。我們僅向    閣下（作為整體）報告，
made solely to you, as a body, and for no other purpose. We do         除此以外，本報告並無其他用途。我們不會
not assume responsibility towards or accept liability to any other     就本報告的內容向任何其他人士負上或承擔
person for the contents of this report.                                任何責任。


Reasonable assurance is a high level of assurance, but is not a        合理鑒證是高水平的鑒證，但不能保證按照
guarantee that an audit conducted in accordance with HKSAs             香港審計準則進行的審計，在某一重大錯誤
will always detect a material misstatement when it exists.             陳述存在時總能發現。錯誤陳述可以由欺詐
Misstatements can arise from fraud or error and are considered         或錯誤引起，如果合理預期它們單獨或匯總
material if, individually or in the aggregate, they could reasonably   起來可能影響綜合財務報表使用者依賴綜合
be expected to influence the economic decisions of users taken         財務報表所作出的經濟決定，則有關的錯誤
on the basis of these consolidated financial statements.               陳述可被視作重大。


As part of an audit in accordance with HKSAs, we exercise              在根據香港審計準則進行審計的過程中，我
professional judgement and maintain professional scepticism            們運用了專業判斷，保持了專業懷疑態度。
throughout the audit. We also:                                         我們亦：


•      Identify and assess the risks of material misstatement          •   識別和評估由於欺詐或錯誤而導致綜
       of the consolidated financial statements, whether due               合財務報表存在重大錯誤陳述的風
       to fraud or error, design and perform audit procedures              險，設計及執行審計程序以應對這些
       responsive to those risks, and obtain audit evidence that           風險，以及獲取充足和適當的審計憑
       is sufficient and appropriate to provide a basis for our            證，作為我們意見的基礎。由於欺詐
       opinion. The risk of not detecting a material misstatement          可能涉及串謀、偽造、蓄意遺漏、虛
       resulting from fraud is higher than for one resulting from          假陳述，或凌駕於內部控制之上，因
       error, as fraud may involve collusion, forgery, intentional         此未能發現因欺詐而導致的重大錯誤
       omissions, misrepresentations, or the override of internal          陳述的風險高於未能發現因錯誤而導
       control.                                                            致的重大錯誤陳述的風險。


•      Obtain an understanding of internal control relevant to         •   了解與審計相關的內部控制，以設計
       the audit in order to design audit procedures that are              適用於多種情況的審計程序，但目的

2.     ACCOUNTING POLICIES                                               2.    會計政策
2.1    BASIS OF PREPARATION                                              2.1   編製基準
       These financial statements have been prepared in                        本財務報表乃根據國際財務報告會計
       accordance with IFRS Accounting Standards, which                        準則編製，而國際財務報告會計準則
       comprise all standards and interpretations as issued by                 包括由國際會計準則理事會（「國際會
       the International Accounting Standards Board (the “IASB”),              計準則理事會」）及國際會計準則及詮
       and International Accounting Standards and Standing                     釋常務委員會批准的準則及詮釋以及
       Interpretations Committee interpretations approved by                   香港公司條例的披露規定。
       the International Accounting Standards Committee and
       the disclosure requirements of the Hong Kong Companies
       Ordinance.


       The consolidated financial information has been prepared                綜合財務資料根據歷史成本慣例編
       under the historical cost convention, except for financial              製，但按公允價值計入損益的金融資
       assets at fair value through profit or loss and equity                  產及指定按公允價值計入其他全面收
       investments designated at fair value through other                      益（「其他全面收益」）的股權投資除
       comprehensive income (“OCl”) which have been measured                   外，其乃按公允價值計量。綜合財務
       at fair value. The consolidated financial statements are                報表以人民幣（「人民幣」）呈列，除另
       presented in Renminbi (“RMB”) and all values are rounded                有指明外，所有金額均約整至最接近
       to the nearest thousand except when otherwise indicated.                的千位數。


       Basis of consolidation                                                  綜合基準

       The consolidated financial statements include the                       綜合財務報表包括本公司及其附屬公
       financial statements of the Company and its subsidiaries                司於截至2025 年12 月31 日止年度之
       for the year ended 31 December 2025. A subsidiary is an                 財務報表。附屬公司指受本公司直
       entity (including a structured entity), directly or indirectly,         接或間接控制之實體（包括結構性實
       controlled by the Company. Control is achieved when the                 體）。倘本集團透過參與被投資方業
       Group is exposed, or has rights, to variable returns from its           務而享有或有權取得可變回報，且有
       involvement with the investee and has the ability to affect             能力行使對被投資方之權力影響有關
       those returns through its power over the investee (i.e.,                回報，則本集團擁有該實體之控制權
       existing rights that give the Group the current ability to              （即現時賦予本集團指導被投資方相關
       direct the relevant activities of the investee).                        活動之能力）。

             Generally, there is a presumption that a majority of voting            一般來說，有一種假設，即多數投票
             rights results in control. When the Company has less than              權產生控制權。倘本公司擁有之被投
             a majority of the voting or similar rights of an investee, the         資方投票權或類似權利不及大半，則
             Group considers all relevant facts and circumstances in                評估本公司對被投資方是否擁有權力
             assessing whether it has power over an investee, including:            時，本集團會考慮所有相關事實及情
                                                                                    況，包括：


             (a)    the contractual arrangement with the other vote                 (a)   與該被投資方其他投票權持有
                    holders of the investee;                                              人之合約安排；


             (b)    rights arising from other contractual arrangements;             (b)   根據其他合約安排產生之權
                    and                                                                   利；及


             (c)    the Group’s voting rights and potential voting rights.          (c)   本集團之投票權及潛在投票
                                                                                          權。


             The financial statements of the subsidiaries are prepared              附屬公司與本公司之財務報表之報告
             for the same reporting period as the Company, using                    期間相同，並採用一致之會計政策編
             consistent accounting policies. The results of subsidiaries            製。附屬公司之業績由本集團獲得控
             are consolidated from the date on which the Group obtains              制權當日起作綜合入賬，直至有關控
             control, and continue to be consolidated until the date that           制權終止當日為止。
             such control ceases.


             Profit or loss and each component of other comprehensive               損益及其他全面收益之各個組成部分
             income are attributed to the owners of the parent of the               歸屬於本集團母公司之擁有人及非控
             Group and to the non-controlling interests, even if this               股權益，即使此舉會導致非控股權益
             results in the non-controlling interests having a deficit              有虧絀結餘。所有有關本集團各成員
             balance. All intra-group assets and liabilities, equity,               公司間之交易之集團內部公司間資產
             income, expenses and cash flows relating to transactions               及負債、權益、收入、開支及現金流
             between members of the Group are eliminated in full on                 量會於綜合計算時全數抵銷。
             consolidation.

       and any resulting surplus or deficit in profit or loss. The            何投資之公允價值及損益中任何因此

       other comprehensive income is reclassified to profit or loss           收益內確認的本集團應佔部分按本集
       or retained profits, as appropriate, on the same basis as              團直接出售相關資產或負債所要求的
       would be required if the Group had directly disposed of the            相同基準重新分類為損益或保留利潤

       DISCLOSURES
       The Group has adopted amendments to IAS 21 Lack of                     本集團已首次就本年度財務報表採納

       financial statements. The Group has not early adopted any              換性」修訂。本集團未提早採納任何
       other standard or amendment that has been issued but is                其他已頒佈但尚未生效的準則或修訂。
       not yet effective.


       Amendments to IAS 21 specify how an entity shall                       對《國際會計準則第21號》修訂訂明，
       assess whether a currency is exchangeable into another                 實體應如何評估一種貨幣能否兌換為
       currency and how it shall estimate a spot exchange rate                另一種貨幣，以及當缺乏可兌換性
       at a measurement date when exchangeability is lacking.                 時，應如何在計量日期估計即期匯
       The amendments require disclosures of information                      率。該等修訂要求披露信息，使財務
       that enable users of financial statements to understand                報表使用者能夠理解貨幣不可兌換的
       the impact of a currency not being exchangeable. As                    影響。由於本集團曾交易的貨幣及海
       the currencies that the Group had transacted in and                    外附屬公司用於折算為本集團列報貨
       the functional currencies of an overseas subsidiary for                幣的功能貨幣均可兌換，故該等修訂
       translation into the Group’s presentation currency were                對本集團財務報表並無任何影響。
       exchangeable, the amendments did not have any impact
       on the Group’s financial statements.

             climate-related examples. Therefore, the amendments do             例反映相應國際財務報告準則會計準
             not have an effective date or transitional provisions.             則中現有要求，即使用氣候相關示例
                                                                                報告財務報表中不確定性的影響。因
                                                                                此，該等修訂無生效日期或過渡條文。

2.4    MATERIAL ACCOUNTING POLICIES                                 2.4   重大會計政策
       Investments in associates                                          於聯營公司的投資

       An associate is an entity in which the Group has a long            聯營公司是指本集團長期持有其不少
       term interest of generally not less than 20% of the equity         於20%投票權且對其擁有重大影響力
       voting rights and over which it has significant influence.         的實體。重大影響力是指有權參與被
       Significant influence is the power to participate in the           投資公司的財務及經營政策的決策，
       financial and operating policy decisions of the investee,          但並非控制或共同控制該等政策。
       but is not control or joint control over those policies.


       The Group’s investments in associates are stated in                本集團於聯營公司的投資乃按權益會
       the consolidated statement of financial position at the            計法，在綜合財務狀況表內，按本集
       Group’s share of net assets under the equity method of             團應佔的資產淨值扣除任何減值虧損
       accounting, less any impairment losses. The Group’s share          呈列。本集團應佔聯營公司收購後業
       of the post-acquisition results and other comprehensive            績及其他全面收入份額分別計入綜合
       income of associates is included in the consolidated               損益表。此外，倘於聯營公司的權益
       statement of profit or loss. In addition, when there has           內直接確認一項變動，則本集團會於
       been a change recognised directly in the equity of the             綜合權益變動表內確認其於任何有關
       associate, the Group recognises its share of any changes,          變動的應佔份額（倘適用）。本集團與
       when applicable, in the consolidated statement of changes          聯營公司交易所產生的未變現盈虧，
       in equity. Unrealised gains and losses resulting from              均按本集團於聯營公司的投資為限進
       transactions between the Group and its associates are              行抵銷，除非未變現虧損證明已轉讓
       eliminated to the extent of the Group’s investments in             資產出現減值則除外。收購聯營公司
       the associates, except where unrealised losses provide             產生的商譽計入本集團於聯營公司的
       evidence of an impairment of the assets transferred.               投資的一部分。
       Goodwill arising from the acquisition of associates is
       included as part of the Group’s investments in associates.

             Business combinations and goodwill                                   業務合併及商譽

             Business combinations are accounted for using the                    業務合併乃以收購法列賬。轉讓對價
             acquisition method. The consideration transferred is                 乃以收購日期的公允價值計量，該公
             measured at the acquisition date fair value which is                 允價值為本集團轉讓的資產於收購日
             the sum of the acquisition date fair values of assets                期的公允價值、本集團向被收購方前
             transferred by the Group, liabilities assumed by the                 擁有人承擔的負債，及本集團發行以
             Group to the former owners of the acquiree and the equity            換取被收購方控制權的股本權益的總
             interests issued by the Group in exchange for control of the         和。於各業務合併中，本集團選擇是
             acquiree. For each business combination, the Group elects            否以公允價值或被收購方可識別淨資
             whether to measure the non-controlling interests in the              產的應佔比例，計量於被收購方的非
             acquiree at fair value or at the proportionate share of the          控股權益。非控股權益的所有其他組
             acquiree’s identifiable net assets. All other components             成部分均按公允價值計量。收購相關
             of non-controlling interests are measured at fair value.             成本於產生時列為開支。
             Acquisition-related costs are expensed as incurred.


             The Group determines that it has acquired a business                 當所收購的一組活動及資產包括一項
             when the acquired set of activities and assets includes an           資源投入及一項實質過程，而兩者對
             input and a substantive process that together significantly          創造產出的能力有重大貢獻時，本集
             contribute to the ability to create outputs.                         團釐定其已收購一項業務。


             When the Group acquires a business, it assesses the                  當本集團收購一項業務時，會根據合
             financial assets and liabilities assumed for appropriate             約條款、於收購日期的經濟環境及相
             classification and designation in accordance with the                關條件，評估須承擔的金融資產及負
             contractual terms, economic circumstances and pertinent              債，以作出適合的分類及標示，其中
             conditions as at the acquisition date. This includes the             包括將被收購方主合約中的嵌入式衍
             separation of embedded derivatives in host contracts of              生工具進行分離。
             the acquiree.

             less any accumulated impairment losses. Goodwill is                 減值虧損計量。商譽每年進行減值測
             tested for impairment annually or more frequently if                試，或倘有事件或情況變動顯示其賬
             events or changes in circumstances indicate that the                面值可能出現減值，則進行更頻密的
             carrying value may be impaired. The Group performs its              測試。本集團截至12月31日對商譽進

             cash-generating units) is less than the carrying amount,            值，則確認減值虧損。已確認的商譽

             recognised for goodwill is not reversed in a subsequent
             period.

2.4    MATERIAL ACCOUNTING POLICIES                                      2.4   重大會計政策（續）
       (CONTINUED)
       Fair value measurement                                                  公允價值計量

       The Group measures its certain financial assets and equity              本集團於報告期間末按公允價值計量
       investments at fair value at the end of the reporting period.           其若干金融資產及股權投資。公允價
       Fair value is the price that would be received to sell an               值為於計量日期在市場參與者間進行
       asset or paid to transfer a liability in an orderly transaction         的有序交易中出售資產所收取或轉移
       between market participants at the measurement date.                    負債所支付的價格。公允價值計量乃
       The fair value measurement is based on the presumption                  假設出售資產或轉移負債的交易於資
       that the transaction to sell the asset or transfer the                  產或負債的主要市場進行，或在未有
       liability takes place either in the principal market for the            主要市場的情況下，則假設於資產或
       asset or liability, or in the absence of a principal market,            負債的最有利市場進行。主要或最有
       in the most advantageous market for the asset or liability.             利市場必須為本集團可進入的市場。
       The principal or the most advantageous market must be                   資產或負債的公允價值乃基於市場參
       accessible by the Group. The fair value of an asset or a                與者為資產或負債定價時所用的假設
       liability is measured using the assumptions that market                 計量（假設市場參與者以符合彼等最
       participants would use when pricing the asset or liability,             佳經濟利益的方式行事）。
       assuming that market participants act in their economic
       best interest.


       A fair value measurement of a non-financial asset takes                 非金融資產的公允價值計量會計及一
       into account a market participant’s ability to generate                 名市場參與者透過以最大限度使用該
       economic benefits by using the asset in its highest and                 資產達致最佳用途，或透過將資產出
       best use or by selling it to another market participant that            售予將以最大限度使用該資產達致最
       would use the asset in its highest and best use.                        佳用途的另一名市場參與者而產生經
                                                                               濟利益的能力。


       The Group uses valuation techniques that are appropriate                本集團使用適用於不同情況且具備足
       in the circumstances and for which sufficient data are                  夠可用數據以計量公允價值的估值方
       available to measure fair value, maximising the use of                  法，以盡量使用相關可觀察輸入數據
       relevant observable inputs and minimising the use of                    及盡量減少使用不可觀察輸入數據。
       unobservable inputs.

             Level 2   – based on valuation techniques for which the                 第二級 － 按對公允價值計量而言屬
                           lowest level input that is significant to the                 重 大 的 可 觀 察（ 直 接 或 間
                           fair value measurement is observable, either                  接）最低級別輸入數據的估
                           directly or indirectly                                        值方法
             Level 3   – based on valuation techniques for which the                 第三級 － 按對公允價值計量而言屬
                           lowest level input that is significant to the                 重大的不可觀察最低級別
                           fair value measurement is unobservable                        輸入數據的估值方法

             determines whether transfers have occurred between                      對公允價值計量整體而言屬重大的最

       Impairment of non-financial assets                                   非金融資產減值

       Where an indication of impairment exists, or when annual             如有跡象顯示資產出現減值，或須對
       impairment testing for an asset is required (other than              資產進行年度減值測試（存貨、遞延
       inventories, deferred tax assets and financial assets),              所得稅資產及金融資產除外），則會估
       the asset’s recoverable amount is estimated. An asset’s              計該項資產的可收回金額。資產的可
       recoverable amount is the higher of the asset’s or cash-             收回金額按資產或現金產生單位的使
       generating unit’s value in use and its fair value less costs         用價值與其公允價值減出售成本的較
       of disposal, and is determined for an individual asset,              高者計算，並就個別資產釐定，惟該
       unless the asset does not generate cash inflows that                 項資產並無產生很大程度上獨立於其
       are largely independent of those from other assets or                他資產或其他組別資產的現金流入則
       groups of assets, in which case the recoverable amount is            除外，在此情況下，就該項資產所屬
       determined for the cash-generating unit to which the asset           的現金產生單位釐定可收回金額。
       belongs.


       In testing a cash-generating unit for impairment, a                  在對現金產生單位進行減值測試時，
       portion of the carrying amount of a corporate asset (e.g., a         倘能按合理一致基準分配，則部分公
       headquarters building) is allocated to an individual cash-           司資產的賬面值分配至單個現金產生
       generating unit if it can be allocated on a reasonable and           單位，否則將分配至最小現金產生單
       consistent basis or, otherwise, to the smallest group of             位。
       cash-generating units.


       An impairment loss is recognised only if the carrying                減值虧損僅於資產賬面值超逾可收回
       amount of an asset exceeds its recoverable amount. In                金額時確認。於評估使用價值時，估
       assessing value in use, the estimated future cash flows              計未來現金流量以反映當時市場對貨
       are discounted to their present value using a pre-tax                幣時間價值的估計及該項資產的特有
       discount rate that reflects current market assessments of            風險的除稅前貼現率貼現至現值。減
       the time value of money and the risks specific to the asset.         值虧損於其產生期間自損益表內與減
       An impairment loss is charged to the statement of profit             值資產功能一致的該等開支分類內扣
       or loss in the period in which it arises in those expense            除。
       categories consistent with the function of the impaired
       asset.

       Leases                                                                 租賃

       The Group assesses at contract inception whether a                     本集團於合約開始時評估合約是否為
       contract is, or contains, a lease. A contract is, or contains,         租賃或包含租賃。倘合約賦予權利於
       a lease if the contract conveys the right to control the use           一段時間內控制已識別資產的用途以
       of an identified asset for a period of time in exchange for            換取對價，則合約為租賃或包含租賃。
       consideration.


      Group as a lessee                                                       本集團作為承租人

       The Group applies a single recognition and measurement                 本集團對所有租賃（惟短期租賃及低
       approach for all leases, except for short-term leases and              價值資產租賃除外）採取單一確認及
       leases of low-value assets. The Group recognises lease                 計量方法。本集團確認用於支付租賃
       liabilities to make lease payments and right-of-use assets             款項的租賃負債及代表相關資產使用
       representing the right to use the underlying assets.                   權的使用權資產。


       (a)    Right-of-use assets                                             (a)   使用權資產

              Right-of-use assets are recognised at the                             使用權資產乃於租賃開始日期
              commencement date of the lease (that is the date                      （即相關資產可供使用的日期）
              the underlying asset is available for use). Right-of-                 確認。使用權資產按成本減累
              use assets are measured at cost, less accumulated                     計折舊及任何減值虧損計量，
              depreciation and any impairment losses, and                           並就租賃負債的任何重新計量
              adjusted for any remeasurement of lease liabilities.                  作出調整。使用權資產成本包
              The cost of right-of-use assets includes the amount                   括已確認租賃負債金額、初步
              of lease liabilities recognised, initial direct costs                 已產生直接成本及於開始日期
              incurred, and lease payments made at or before                        或之前作出的租賃付款減任何
              the commencement date less any lease incentives                       已收取租賃獎勵。使用權資產
              received. Right-of-use assets are depreciated on                      於資產租期及估計可使用年期
              a straight-line basis over the shorter of the lease                   （ 以 較 短 者 為 準 ）按 直 線 法 折
              terms and the estimated useful lives of the assets                    舊，詳情如下：
              as follows:

              Office premises and plant                                                            2 to 6 years

              Leasehold land                                                                          50 years

       characteristics and the Group’s business model for                     團管理彼等的業務模式。除了並不包
       managing them. With the exception of trade receivables                 含顯著的融資成分或本集團已就此應
       that do not contain a significant financing component or               用實務中的簡易處理方法不調整重大
       for which the Group has applied the practical expedient                融資成分之影響之貿易應收款外，本
       of not adjusting the effect of a significant financing                 集團初始按公允價值加上（倘金融資

       at its fair value plus in the case of a financial asset not at         本計量金融資產。並不包含顯著的融
       fair value through profit or loss, transaction costs. Trade            資成分或本集團已就此應用實務中的
       receivables that do not contain a significant financing                簡易處理方法之貿易應收款，乃根據
       component or for which the Group has applied the                       國際財務報告準則第 15 號按照下文
       practical expedient are measured at the transaction price              「收益確認」所載之政策釐定之交易價
       determined under IFRS 15 in accordance with the policies               格計量。
       set out for “Revenue recognition” below.


       In order for a financial asset to be classified and                    就按攤銷成本或按公允價值計入其他

             The Group’s business model for managing financial assets             本集團管理金融資產的業務模式指其
             refers to how it manages its financial assets in order to            如何管理其金融資產以產生現金流
             generate cash flows. The business model determines                   量。業務模式確定現金流量是否來自
             whether cash flows will result from collecting contractual           收取合約現金流量、出售金融資產，
             cash flows, selling the financial assets, or both. Financial         或兩者兼有。按攤銷成本進行分類及
             assets classified and measured at amortised cost are                 計量之金融資產於旨在持有金融資產
             held within a business model with the objective to hold              以收取合約現金流量的業務模式中持
             financial assets in order to collect contractual cash flows,         有，而按公允價值計入其他全面收入
             while financial assets classified and measured at fair               進行分類及計量之金融資產於旨在持
             value through other comprehensive income are held within             有金融資產以收取合約現金流量及出
             a business model with the objective of both holding to               售金融資產的業務模式中持有。未於
             collect contractual cash flows and selling. Financial assets         上述業務模式中持有的金融資產按公
             which are not held within the aforementioned business                允價值計入損益進行分類及計量。
             models are classified and measured at fair value through
             profit or loss.


             Purchases or sales of financial assets that require delivery         須於一般由市場規則或慣例設定的期
             of assets within the period generally established by                 間內交付資產的金融資產買賣，乃於
             regulation or convention in the marketplace are recognised           交易日（即本集團承諾買賣資產當日）
             on the trade date, that is, the date that the Group commits          確認。
             to purchase or sell the asset.

             subject to impairment. Gains and losses are recognised               響。當資產終止確認、修訂或減值

             Impairment of financial assets                                      金融資產減值

             losses (“ECLs”) for all debt instruments not held at                益的所有債務工具預期信貸虧損（「預
             fair value through profit or loss. ECLs are based on the            期信貸虧損」）的撥備。預期信貸虧損

2.4    MATERIAL ACCOUNTING POLICIES                                    2.4   重大會計政策（續）
       (CONTINUED)
       Impairment of financial assets (Continued)                            金融資產減值（續）

      General approach                                                       一般法

       ECLs are recognised in two stages. For credit exposures for           預期信貸虧損分兩個階段進行確認。
       which there has not been a significant increase in credit             就自初步確認起未有顯著增加的信貸
       risk since initial recognition, ECLs are provided for credit          風險而言，預期信貸虧損提供予由未
       losses that result from default events that are possible              來12個月內可能發生違約事件而導致
       within the next 12 months (a 12-month ECL). For those                 的信貸虧損（12個月預期信貸虧損）。
       credit exposures for which there has been a significant               就自初步確認起經已顯著增加的信貸
       increase in credit risk since initial recognition, a loss             風險而言，不論何時發生違約，於餘
       allowance is required for credit losses expected over the             下風險年期內的預期信貸虧損均須計
       remaining life of the exposure, irrespective of the timing of         提虧損準備（年限內預期信貸虧損）。
       the default (a lifetime ECL).


       At each reporting date, the Group assesses whether                    在各報告日期，本集團評估自初步確
       the credit risk on a financial instrument has increased               認起金融工具的信貸風險是否顯著
       significantly since initial recognition. When making the              增加。進行評估時，本集團比較金融
       assessment, the Group compares the risk of a default                  工具在報告日期發生違約的風險及金
       occurring on the financial instrument as at the reporting             融工具在初始確認日期發生違約的風
       date with the risk of a default occurring on the financial            險，同時考慮無需沒有過多的成本和
       instrument as at the date of initial recognition and                  努力即可獲得的合理的和可支持的信
       considers reasonable and supportable information that is              息，包括歷史和前瞻性信息。本集團
       available without undue cost or effort, including historical          認為，當合同付款逾期超過30天時，
       and forward-looking information. The Group considers that             信貸風險會顯著增加。
       there has been a significant increase in credit risk when
       contractual payments are more than 30 days past due.


       The Group considers a financial asset in default when                 當合約付款逾期90天時，本集團將考
       contractual payments are 90 days past due. However, in                慮違約的金融資產。然而，在某些情
       certain cases, the Group may also consider a financial                況下，當內部或外部信息表明本集團
       asset to be in default when internal or external information          不太可能在考慮到本集團所持有的任
       indicates that the Group is unlikely to receive the                   何信貸增強之前全額收到未付合約款
       outstanding contractual amounts in full before taking into            項時，本集團也可將一項金融資產視
       account any credit enhancements held by the Group.                    為違約。


       A financial asset is written off when there is no reasonable          金融資產在沒有合理預期收回合約現
       expectation of recovering the contractual cash flows.                 金流的情況下被沖銷。

2.4    MATERIAL ACCOUNTING POLICIES                                       2.4   重大會計政策（續）
       (CONTINUED)
       Impairment of financial assets (Continued)                               金融資產減值（續）

      Simplified approach                                                       簡化方法

       For trade receivables that do not contain a significant                  對於不包含重要融資組成部分的貿易
       financing component or when the Group applies the                        應收款項，或當本集團採取實務中不
       practical expedient of not adjusting the effect of a                     調整重要融資組成部分的影響時，本
       significant financing component, the Group applies                       集團在計算預期信貸虧損時採用簡易
       the simplified approach in calculating ECLs. Under the                   法。根據簡易法，本集團不跟蹤信貸
       simplified approach, the Group does not track changes                    風險的變化，而是在每個報告日期確
       in credit risk, but instead recognises a loss allowance                  認基於全期預期信貸虧損計算虧損準
       based on lifetime ECLs at each reporting date. The Group                 備。本集團根據其歷史信貸虧損經驗
       has established a provision matrix that is based on its                  建立了一個撥備矩陣，並根據債務人
       historical credit loss experience, adjusted for forward-                 具體的前瞻性因素和經濟環境加以調
       looking factors specific to the debtors and the economic                 整。
       environment.


       Financial liabilities                                                    金融負債

      Initial recognition and measurement                                       初步確認及計量

       Financial liabilities are classified, at initial recognition, as         金融負債於初步確認時被分類為貸款
       loans and borrowings, payables as appropriate.                           及借款、應付款項（如適用）。


       All financial liabilities are recognised initially at fair value         所有金融負債初步按公允價值確認，
       and, in the case of loans and borrowings and payables, net               倘為貸款及借款及應付款項，則應扣
       of directly attributable transaction costs.                              除直接應佔交易成本。

2.4    MATERIAL ACCOUNTING POLICIES                                     2.4   重大會計政策（續）
       (CONTINUED)
       Derecognition of financial liabilities                                 終止確認金融負債

       A financial liability is derecognised when the obligation              當負債之責任解除或註銷或到期時，
       under the liability is discharged or cancelled, or expires.            金融負債將終止確認。


       When an existing financial liability is replaced by another            若現有金融負債由另一項來自相同貸
       from the same lender on substantially different terms,                 方按完全不同之條款提供負債取代，
       or the terms of an existing liability are substantially                或現有負債之條款作出重大修訂，則
       modified, such an exchange or modification is treated as               上述取代或修訂視為終止確認原有負
       a derecognition of the original liability and a recognition of         債及確認新負債，而相關賬面值之差
       a new liability, and the difference between the respective             額在損益表確認。
       carrying amounts is recognised in the statement of profit
       or loss.


       Inventories                                                            存貨

       Inventories are stated at the lower of cost and net                    存貨按成本與可變現淨值兩者中的較
       realisable value. Cost is determined on the weighted                   低者列賬。成本以加權平均法計算，
       average method and, in the case of work in progress and                而就在製品及製成品而言，成本包括
       finished goods, comprises direct materials, direct labour              直接材料、直接勞工及適當比例的間
       and an appropriate proportion of overheads. Net realisable             接成本。可變現淨值按估計售價減完
       value is based on estimated selling prices less any                    成及出售將產生的任何估計成本計算。
       estimated costs to be incurred to completion and disposal.


       Cash and cash equivalents                                              現金及現金等價物

       Cash and cash equivalents in the statement of financial                財務狀況表中的現金及現金等價物包
       position comprise cash on hand and at banks, and short-                括手頭及銀行現金，以及到期日一般
       term highly liquid deposits with a maturity of generally               為三個月內、可隨時兌換為可知數額
       within three months that are readily convertible into                  之現金、毋須承受價值變動之重大風
       known amounts of cash, subject to an insignificant risk                險且為履行短期現金承諾而持有的短
       of changes in value and held for the purpose of meeting                期高流動性存款。
       short-term cash commitments.


       For the purpose of the consolidated statement of cash                  就綜合現金流量表而言，現金及現金
       flows, cash and cash equivalents comprise cash on hand                 等價物包括用途不受限制的手頭及銀
       and at banks, including short-term deposits, and assets                行現金（包括短期存款）以及性質類似
       similar in nature to cash, which are not restricted as to              現金的資產。
       use.

             Provisions                                                           撥備

             A provision is recognised when a present obligation (legal           倘若因過往事件導致現有債務（法定
             or constructive) has arisen as a result of a past event and          或推定），且日後很可能需要有資源流
             it is probable that a future outflow of resources will be            出以償還債務，則確認撥備，但必須
             required to settle the obligation, provided that a reliable          能可靠估計有關債務金額。
             estimate can be made of the amount of the obligation.


             When the effect of discounting is material, the amount               倘若貼現的影響重大，則確認的撥備
             recognised for a provision is the present value at the end           金額為預期需用作償還債務的未來支
             of the reporting period of the future expenditures expected          出於報告期末的現值。因時間流逝而
             to be required to settle the obligation. The increase in the         產生的貼現現值增加部分，列作財務
             discounted present value amount arising from the passage             成本計入損益表內。
             of time is included in finance costs in the statement of
             profit or loss.


             Income tax                                                           所得稅

             Income tax comprises current and deferred tax. Income                所得稅包括當期及遞延稅項。有關在
             tax relating to items recognised outside profit or loss              損益以外確認項目之所得稅在損益以
             is recognised outside profit or loss, either in other                外確認，計入其他全面收益或直接計
             comprehensive income or directly in equity.                          入權益內。


             Current tax assets and liabilities are measured at the               當期稅項資產或負債按預期可獲稅務
             amount expected to be recovered from or paid to the                  當局退回或付予稅務當局之金額，根
             taxation authorities, based on tax rates (and tax laws)              據報告期末已實施或已實際實施之稅
             that have been enacted or substantively enacted by the               率（及稅法）計算，而計算時亦考慮到
             end of the reporting period, taking into consideration               本集團經營所在國家現行之詮釋及慣
             interpretations and practices prevailing in the countries in         例。
             which the Group operates.


             Deferred tax is provided, using the liability method, on all         遞延稅項為就財務申報而言，於報告
             temporary differences at the end of the reporting period             期末就資產及負債之稅基及彼等賬面
             between the tax bases of assets and liabilities and their            值之所有暫時性差異，利用負債法作
             carrying amounts for financial reporting purposes.                   出撥備。

       tax rates that are expected to apply to the period when              末已實施或已實際實施之稅率（及稅
       the asset is realised or the liability is settled, based             法），按變現資產或清償負債之期間預
       on tax rates (and tax laws) that have been enacted or                期適用之稅率予以計量。
       substantively enacted by the end of the reporting period.

       Deferred tax assets and deferred tax liabilities are offset          當且僅當：本集團存在可依法強制執
       if and only if the Group has a legally enforceable right to          行的權利，可將即期稅項資產與負債
       set off current tax assets and current tax liabilities and           相抵銷；與所得稅相關之遞延稅項資
       the deferred tax assets and deferred tax liabilities relate          產與負債涉及同一應課稅實體及同一
       to income taxes levied by the same taxation authority on             稅務機關，或涉及本集團內不同應課
       either the same taxable entity or different taxable entities         稅實體（同一稅務機關），該等實體計
       which intend either to settle current tax liabilities and            劃將即期稅項資產與負債按淨額基準
       assets on a net basis, or to realise the assets and settle           相抵銷，或在未來預計變現重大遞延
       the liabilities simultaneously, in each future period in             稅項資產或清償遞延稅項負債的每一
       which significant amounts of deferred tax liabilities or             會計期間，同時變現資產或清償負債
       assets are expected to be settled or recovered.                      時，遞延稅項資產及遞延稅項負債方
                                                                            可互相抵銷。


       Treasury shares                                                      庫存股份

       Own equity instruments which are reacquired and held by              本公司或本集團購回及持有本身的權
       the Company or the Group (treasury shares) are recognised            益工具（庫存股份）按成本直接於權益
       directly in equity at cost. No gain or loss is recognised in         確認。因購買、出售、發行或註銷本
       the statement of profit or loss on the purchase, sale, issue         集團本身的權益工具產生的收益或損
       or cancellation of the Group’s own equity instruments.               失不會在損益表確認。

       Government grants                                                    政府補助

       Government grants are recognised at their fair value                 倘能合理肯定能收到補助及遵循補助
       where there is reasonable assurance that the grant will be           的所有相關條件，政府補助會按公允
       received and all attaching conditions will be complied with,         價值確認。倘補助與開支項目相關，
       When the grant relates to an expense item, it is recognised          會有系統地將補助配對擬補償的成
       as income on a systematic basis over the periods that the            本，於所需期間確認為收入。
       costs, for which it is intended to compensate are expensed.

       Where the grant relates to an asset, the fair value is               倘補助與資產相關，其公允價值會被
       credited to a deferred income account and is released to             貸記至遞延收入賬戶，並於相關資產
       the statement of profit or loss over the expected useful             的預期使用年限內，按等額年度分期
       life of the relevant asset by equal annual instalments               或從資產賬面值中扣除通過減少折舊
       or deducted from the carrying amount of the asset and                支出的方式，結轉至損益表。
       released to the statement of profit or loss by way of a
       reduced depreciation charge.

             those goods or services.


             When the consideration in a contract includes a variable            當合約中的對價包含可變金額時，對
             amount, the amount of consideration is estimated to which           價金額於本集團向客戶轉讓貨品或服
             the Group will be entitled in exchange for transferring             務而有權獲得交換時估計。可變對價
             the goods or services to the customer. The variable                 於合約開始時估計並受到約束，直至
             consideration is estimated at contract inception and                與可變對價相關的不確定因素得到解
             constrained until it is highly probable that a significant          決時，確認的累計收入金額極有可能
             revenue reversal in the amount of cumulative revenue                不會發生重大收入回撥。
             recognised will not occur when the associated uncertainty
             with the variable consideration is subsequently resolved.


             When the contract contains a financing component                    當合約中包含融資成分，該融資成分
             which provides the customer with a significant benefit              為客戶提供超過一年的貨品或服務轉
             of financing the transfer of goods or services to the               讓融資的重大利益時，收入按應收款
             customer for more than one year, revenue is measured at             項的現值計量，使用貼現率折現，該
             the present value of the amount receivable, discounted              貼現率將反映在本集團與客戶在合約
             using the discount rate that would be reflected in a                開始時的單獨融資交易中。當合約中
             separate financing transaction between the Group and                包含融資部分，該融資部分為本集團
             the customer at contract inception. When the contract               提供了一年以上的重大財務利益時，
             contains a financing component which provides the Group             合約項下確認的收入包括按實際利率
             with a significant financial benefit for more than one              法在合約負債上加算的利息。就客戶
             year, revenue recognised under the contract includes the            付款至轉讓承諾貨品或者服務的期限
             interest expense accreted on the contract liability under           為一年或者更短的合約而言，交易價
             the effective interest method. For a contract where the             格採用國際財務報告準則第15號中實
             period between the payment by the customer and the                  際權宜之計，不會對重大融資部分的
             transfer of the promised goods or services is one year or           影響作出調整。
             less, the transaction price is not adjusted for the effects
             of a significant financing component, using the practical
             expedient in IFRS 15.

       Contract liabilities                                                合約負債

       A contract liability is recognised when a payment is                合約負債於本集團轉移相關貨品或服
       received or a payment is due (whichever is earlier) from            務前收取客戶付款或付款到期（以較
       a customer before the Group transfers the related goods             早發生者為準）時確認。合約負債於
       or services. Contract liabilities are recognised as revenue         本集團履行合約（即將相關貨品或服
       when the Group performs under the contract (i.e., transfers         務的控制權轉移至客戶）時確認為收
       control of the related goods or services to the customer).          益。

       changes in the forward-looking estimates are analysed.               析。

             The Group is subject to income tax on an entity basis on              本集團須按實體基準就產生自或源自
             profits arising in or derived from the jurisdictions in which         本集團成員公司註冊及營運所在司法
             members of the Group are domiciled and operate.                       權區的利潤繳納所得稅。


            PRC corporate income tax                                               中國企業所得稅

             Under the Law of the PRC on Enterprise Income Tax (the                根據中國企業所得稅法（「企業所得稅
             “EIT Law”) and Implementation Regulation of the EIT Law,              法」）及企業所得稅法實施條例，除免

      32.    RESERVES                                                        32.   儲備
             The amounts of the Group’s reserves and the movements                 本集團的儲備及其變動於財務報表的
             therein are presented in the consolidated statements of               綜合權益變動表內呈列。
             changes in equity in financial statements.


             (i)    Statutory reserve                                              (i)    法定儲備

                    In accordance with the Company Law of the PRC,                        根據中國公司法，在中國註冊
                    companies registered in the PRC are required to                       的公司須將法定除稅後利潤的
                    allocate 10% of the statutory after tax profits to the                10%轉撥至法定儲備，直至儲
                    statutory reserve until the cumulative total of the                   備的累計總額達到公司註冊資
                    reserve reaches 50% of the companies registered                       本的50%。待中國有關當局批
                    capital. Subject to approval from the relevant PRC                    准後，法定儲備可用於抵銷任
                    authorities, the statutory reserve may be used                        何累計虧損或增加公司的註冊
                    to offset any accumulated losses or increase the                      資本。法定儲備不可用於向中
                    registered capital of the companies. The statutory                    國附屬公司權益持有人分派股
                    reserve is not available for dividend distribution to                 息。
                    equity holders of the PRC subsidiaries.


             (ii)   Capital reserve                                                (ii)   資本儲備

                    the excess of the consideration received for                          度就認購本公司註冊資本所收

39.    FAIR VALUE AND FAIR VALUE HIERARCHY OF                         39.   金融工具的公允價值及公允價
       FINANCIAL INSTRUMENTS                                                值等級
       Management has assessed that the fair values of cash and             管理層已評定現金及銀行結餘、受限
       bank balances, restricted cash, trade receivables, financial         制現金、貿易應收款項、計入預付款
       assets included in prepayments, other receivables and                項、其他應收款項及其他資產的金融
       other assets, interest-bearing bank and other borrowings,            資產、計息銀行及其他借款、貿易應
       trade payables, financial liabilities included in other              付款項、計入其他應付款項及應計費
       payables and accruals approximate to their carrying                  用的金融負債的公允價值與其賬面值
       amounts largely due to the short-term maturities of these            相若，主要由於該等工具的短期到期
       instruments.                                                         情況所致。


       The Group’s corporate finance team is responsible for                本集團的企業財務團隊負責釐定金融
       determining the policies and procedures for the fair value           工具公允價值管理的政策及程序。企
       management of financial instruments. The corporate                   業財務團隊直接向財務總監及董事會
       finance team reports directly to the chief financial officer         匯報。於各報告日期，企業財務團隊
       and the board of directors. At each reporting date, the              分析金融工具價值的變動，並釐定估
       corporate finance team analyses the movements in the                 值所應用的主要輸入數據。估值由財
       values of financial instruments and determines the major             務總監審核及批准。估值過程及結果
       inputs applied in the valuation. The valuation is reviewed           乃與董事會討論以作出年度財務報告。
       and approved by the chief financial officer. The valuation
       process and results are discussed with the board of
       directors for annual financial reporting.


       The fair values of the financial assets and liabilities are          金融資產及負債的公允價值按自願訂
       included at the amount at which the instrument could be              約方（強迫或清盤出售除外）當前交易
       exchanged in a current transaction between willing parties,          中該工具的可交換金額入賬。在估計
       other than in a forced or liquidation sale. The following            其公允價值時已採用下列方法及假設。
       methods and assumptions were used to estimate the fair
       values.


       The fair value of long-term bank deposits has been                   長期銀行存款的公允價值乃採用具有
       calculated by discounting the expected future cash flows             類似條款、信貸風險及剩餘到期日的
       using rates currently available for instruments with similar         工具當前適用的現行利率貼現預期未
       terms, credit risk and remaining maturities. The carrying            來現金流量計算。長期銀行存款的賬
       amounts of long-term bank deposits approximate to their              面值與其公允價值相若。
       fair values.

      40.    FINANCIAL RISK MANAGEMENT OBJECTIVES                            40.   財務風險管理目標及政策
             AND POLICIES
             The Group’s principal financial instruments comprise bank             本集團的主要金融工具包括銀行及其
             and other interest-bearing borrowings, long-term bank                 他計息借款、長期銀行存款以及現金
             deposits and cash and bank balances. The main purpose                 及銀行結餘。該等金融工具的主要目
             of these financial instruments is to raise finance for the            的是為本集團的營運籌集資金。本集
             Group’s operations. The Group has various other financial             團有多項其他金融資產及負債，如直

       rate. The directors of the Company consider the Group’s               為本集團就銀行結餘、長期銀行存款

       long-term bank deposits and interest-bearing bank and                 風險並不重大，乃因大部分該等款項
       other borrowings is not significant as most of them are at            按固定利率計息。
       fixed interest rates.

       third parties. It is the Group’s policy that all customers            進行交易。本集團的政策為所有欲按
       who wish to trade on credit terms are subject to credit               信貸期進行交易的客戶均須通過信貸

       are monitored on an ongoing basis and the Group’s                     收款項結餘，而本集團所面對的壞賬
       exposure to bad debts is not significant.                             風險並不重大。

       Capital management                                                  資本管理

       The primary objectives of the Group’s capital management            本集團資本管理的主要目標是維護本
       are to safeguard the Group’s ability to continue as a going         集團的持續經營能力及維持穩健資本
       concern and to maintain healthy capital ratios in order to          充足率以支持業務發展及實現權益持
       support its business and maximise equity holders’ value.            有人價值最大化。


       The Group manages its capital structure and makes                   本集團會根據經濟狀況變化和相關資
       adjustments to it in light of changes in economic                   產的風險特徵管理或調整資本結構。
       conditions and the risk characteristics of the underlying           為維持或調整資本結構，本集團會調
       assets. To maintain or adjust the capital structure, the            整派發予權益持有人的股息、返還股
       Group may adjust the dividend payment to equity holders,            本予權益持有人或發行新股。本集團
       return capital to equity holders or issue new shares. The           並無受到任何外部資本需求的約束。
       Group is not subject to any externally imposed capital              於截至2025年12月31日及2024年12
       requirements. No changes were made in the objectives,               月31日止年度，本集團並無對資本管
       policies or processes for managing capital during the years         理的目標、政策或程序作出變動。
       ended 31 December 2025 and 31 December 2024.


       The Group monitors capital using a gearing ratio, which             本集團使用資產負債比率監控資本，
       is debt divided by the capital plus debt. Debt includes             資產負債比率為負債除以資本加上負
       interest-bearing borrowings and lease liabilities. Capital          債。負債包括計息借款和租賃負債。
       represents equity attributable to owners of the parent. The         資本指母公司擁有人應佔權益。於報
       gearing ratios as at the end of the reporting periods were          告期末的資產負債比率如下：
       as follows:
